{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01BwThbE4TifLViEU9dg2Hq1","type":"tool_result","content":"# renewal_risk changelog\n\n## 2.4.1 - 2026-03-10\n- scoring: score date is interpreted in UTC (the scheduler runs in UTC; the job previously used the\n  container's local date).\n\n## 2.4.0 - 2026-02-16 (model renewal-risk-v2.4, deployed 2026-03-02)\n- sources: CRM and Customer Success readers migrated to CRM v3 (RA-DS-298) ahead of the snapshot\n  table decommissioning on 2026-01-31.\n- features: new `open_expansion_opps` (open expansion pipeline on the account) (RA-DS-302).\n- model: `class_weight = \"balanced\"` to improve recall on churners (RA-DS-311).\n- model: quantile winsorization (p1/p99, learned on the training split) replaces hand-set caps (RA-DS-305).\n- examples: training window extended from 2 to 3 years of settled renewals (RA-DS-307).\n- deps: scikit-learn 1.3.2 -> 1.5.2, pandas 2.1.4 -> 2.2.3, numpy 1.26.4 -> 2.1.3.\n\n## 2.3.4 - 2025-08-18 (model renewal-risk-v2.3)\n- Q3 2025 retrain. No code changes besides dependency patch releases.\n\n## 2.3.0 - 2025-02-10\n- features: support ticket features (`tickets_90d`, `sev1_tickets_180d`, `open_tickets_at_prediction`).\n- features: CRM pipeline and Customer Success health features (RA-DS-240).\n\n## 2.0.0 - 2024-05-06\n- First production model: logistic regression on contract and product-usage features, scored 90 days\n  before renewal.\n# Model card - renewal-risk\n\nOwner: Revenue Data Science. Consumers: Customer Success (save plays), Sales (renewal forecasting),\nRevOps (capacity planning). Current production model: `renewal-risk-v2.4` (since 2026-03-02).\n\n## Intended use\n\nRank contracts coming up for renewal by their risk of churning so that Customer Success can\nintervene early. Scores are produced 90 days before the renewal date; interventions (executive\nsponsor, success plan, commercial concessions) take most of that window.\n\n## Prediction point\n\nThe production scoring job runs every day at 00:30 UTC. For score date `D` it scores every contract\nwhose renewal date is `D + 90 days` and whose renewal has not been decided, using the warehouse as it\nstands at the start of `D` (00:00 UTC). A contract is scored exactly once.\n\nTraining and offline evaluation reproduce this: each example is a past renewal scored at its\nprediction date (`renewal_date - 90 days`).\n\n## Label\n\n`label = 1` when the renewal outcome is `churned`, `0` when `renewed` (`renewal_outcomes`).\n\n## Examples and split\n\n- Eligible: renewals whose outcome is in the warehouse at the training run date (`--as-of`) and whose\n  renewal date is at least `label_grace_days` (30) before it.\n- Evaluation (`split = eval`): the most recent `eval_window_days` (270) of eligible renewal dates.\n- Training (`split = train`): the preceding `train_window_days` (1,095) of renewal dates.\n- One example per contract renewal. Examples are never filtered on feature values.\n\n## Model\n\nscikit-learn pipeline, fitted on the training split only:\n\n1. Quantile winsorization per feature (p1/p99 learned on the training split).\n2. Standardization.\n3. L2-regularized logistic regression (`C = 0.5`, `class_weight = \"balanced\"`, lbfgs).\n\nFeatures and their order: `src/renewal_risk/features/registry.py`; definitions:\n`docs/feature_dictionary.md`. Hyperparameters: `config/pipeline.toml`.\n\n## Evaluation\n\n`reports/model_evaluation/eval_<as_of>.json`: ROC AUC, PR AUC, Brier score, log loss, calibration by\nscore decile, ROC AUC by segment - all computed from `artifacts/eval_predictions.csv`.\n\n## Monitoring\n\n`reports/monitoring/`: daily production scores and live performance once renewals are decided.\n\n## Version history\n\n| Version | Trained as of | Deployed | Notes |\n|---------|---------------|----------|-------|\n| v2.0 | 2024-04-15 | 2024-05-06 | contract + usage features |\n| v2.3 | 2025-08-15 | 2025-08-18 | + support, CRM pipeline and CS health features (daily snapshot tables) |\n| v2.4 | 2026-02-15 | 2026-03-02 | CRM v3 readers, expansion pipeline feature, balanced class weights, winsorization, 3-year window |\n# Feature dictionary - renewal-risk\n\nEvery feature describes the account at the example's prediction time (see the model card,\n\"Prediction point\"). Values are written to `artifacts/features.csv` exactly as defined here; model\npreprocessing (winsorization, scaling) happens inside the model pipeline.\n\nNotation: `P` = prediction date (00:00 UTC), `R` = renewal date, `arr`, `seats` = the renewing contract's.\n\n## Contract\n\n| Feature | Definition | Source |\n|---------|------------|--------|\n| `arr_usd_log` | `ln(1 + arr)` | `contracts` |\n| `seats_licensed` | seats on the renewing contract | `contracts` |\n| `plan_enterprise` | 1 if plan is `Enterprise` | `contracts` |\n| `tenure_months` | days from account creation date to `P`, divided by 30.4375 | `accounts` |\n| `prior_renewals` | renewals of the account decided as `renewed` and loaded before `P` | `renewal_outcomes` |\n\n## Product usage\n\nWeekly rollups are loaded the morning after the week closes; a week is used when\n`week_start + 7 days < P`.\n\n| Feature | Definition | Default (no weeks) |\n|---------|------------|--------------------|\n| `active_user_ratio_4w` | mean `active_users` over the last 4 usable weeks / `seats` | 0.0 |\n| `active_user_trend_12w` | least-squares slope of `active_users / seats` against week index over the last 12 usable weeks (oldest = 0); 0.0 with fewer than 3 weeks | 0.0 |\n| `api_calls_4w_log` | `ln(1 + sum api_calls)` over the last 4 usable weeks | 0.0 |\n| `logins_per_active_user_4w` | `sum logins / max(1, sum active_users)` over the last 4 usable weeks | 0.0 |\n\n## Support\n\n| Feature | Definition |\n|---------|------------|\n| `tickets_90d` | tickets opened before `P` and at most 90 days before `P` |\n| `sev1_tickets_180d` | `sev1` tickets opened before `P` and at most 180 days before `P` |\n| `open_tickets_at_prediction` | tickets opened before `P` that were not closed before `P` |\n\n## Sales pipeline (CRM)\n\nThe renewal opportunity is the opportunity of type `renewal` on the renewing contract. When there\nis no renewal opportunity for the example, the defaults apply.\n\n| Feature | Definition | Default |\n|---------|------------|---------|\n| `has_renewal_opp` | 1 if there is a renewal opportunity | 0 |\n| `renewal_stage_ordinal` | stage mapped Closed Lost=0, Qualification=1, Discovery=2, Proposal=3, Negotiation=4, Verbal=5, Closed Won=6 | -1 |\n| `forecast_commit` | 1 if forecast category is `Commit` | 0 |\n| `forecast_best_case` | 1 if forecast category is `Best Case` | 0 |\n| `forecast_omitted` | 1 if forecast category is `Omitted` | 0 |\n| `renewal_amount_ratio` | opportunity amount / `arr` | 1.0 |\n| `days_to_opp_close` | days from `P` to the opportunity close date | 90 |\n| `competitor_flagged` | 1 if a competitor is recorded on the opportunity | 0 |\n| `open_expansion_opps` | expansion opportunities on the account that are not in a closed stage (`Closed Won`, `Closed Lost`) | 0 |\n\n## Customer Success health\n\nWhen the account has no health record, the defaults apply.\n\n| Feature | Definition | Default |\n|---------|------------|---------|\n| `health_score` | account health score (0-100) | 50.0 |\n| `health_red` | 1 if health color is `Red` | 0 |\n| `nps_last` | most recent NPS response recorded on the account; missing NPS counts as the default | 0.0 |\n| `csm_sentiment_negative` | 1 if the CSM sentiment is `Negative` | 0 |\n# renewal_risk training pipeline configuration.\n# Paths are relative to the workspace root (the parent of this config/ directory).\n\n[paths]\nwarehouse_db = \"data/warehouse.db\"\nartifacts_dir = \"artifacts\"\nreports_dir = \"reports\"\n\n[run]\n# Default training run date. Current production model: `renewal-risk-v2.4` (since 2026-03-02).\n\n## Intended use\n\nRank contracts coming up for renewal by their risk of churning so that Customer Success can\nintervene early. Scores are produced 90 days before the renewal date; interventions (executive\nsponsor, success plan, commercial concessions) take most of that window.\n\n## Prediction point\n\nThe production scoring job runs every day at 00:30 UTC. For score date `D` it scores every contract\nwhose renewal date is `D + 90 days` and whose renewal has not been decided, using the warehouse as it\nstands at the start of `D` (00:00 UTC). A contract is scored exactly once.\n\nTraining and offline evaluation reproduce this: each example is a past renewal scored at its\nprediction date (`renewal_date - 90 days`).\n\n## Label\n\n`label = 1` when the renewal outcome is `churned`, `0` when `renewed` (`renewal_outcomes`).\n\n## Examples and split\n\n- Eligible: renewals whose outcome is in the warehouse at the training run date (`--as-of`) and whose\n  renewal date is at least `label_grace_days` (30) before it.\n- Evaluation (`split = eval`): the most recent `eval_window_days` (270) of eligible renewal dates.\n- Training (`split = train`): the preceding `train_window_days` (1,095) of renewal dates.\n- One example per contract renewal. Examples are never filtered on feature values.\n\n## Model\n\nscikit-learn pipeline, fitted on the training split only:\n\n1. Scheduler job `renewal_risk_quarterly_retrain` runs `python -m renewal_risk run --config config/pipeline.toml --as-of <quarter date>`.\n2. Owner reviews `reports/model_evaluation/eval_<as_of>.json` against the production model's report:\n   ROC AUC, PR AUC, calibration by decile, AUC by segment.\n3. Promotion needs: no metric regression beyond 0.02 ROC AUC, calibration within +/-5 points per decile\n   band, sign-off from the Customer Success analytics lead.\n4. Promote: copy the model artifact to the scoring service; daily scoring switches the next day.\n5. After go-live, `reports/monitoring/` tracks live performance as renewals are decided (about 90-120\n   days after the first scores).\n# Analytics warehouse - data dictionary (renewal-risk sources)\n\nThe warehouse is a replica fed by connectors. Every table has `synced_at`: the UTC time the row (or\nits latest version) was loaded into the warehouse. Source systems also record their own business\ntimestamps (`changed_at`, `opened_at`, `decided_at`, ...), which can be earlier than `synced_at`.\n\n## Load schedules\n\n| Source | Connector | Typical delay between source commit and `synced_at` |\n|--------|-----------|------------------------------------------------------|\n| Billing contracts | nightly batch | contracts exist before their start date |\n| Renewal outcomes | change stream | minutes |\n| Product usage | weekly rollup job | loaded Monday ~02:00 UTC for the week that closed on Sunday |\n| Support tickets | change stream | minutes |\n| CRM (CRM v3), direct-sales records | change stream | minutes; paused during replication incidents and replayed afterwards (see `warehouse_sync_log`, `docs/ops/`) |\n| CRM (CRM v3), partner-channel records (`lead_source = partner`) | partner integration, weekly batch | up to a week (Sunday 22:00 UTC batch) |\n| Customer Success platform | nightly batch | changes made on day D are loaded ~01:30 UTC on D+1 |\n\nRows loaded by one connector for one record are applied in the order they were committed in the source.\n\n## Tables\n\n### `accounts` - one row per account\n`account_id`, `segment` (Enterprise / Mid-Market / SMB), `region`, `industry`, `created_date`, `synced_at`.\nStatic firmographics.\n\n### `contracts` - one row per contract term\n`contract_id`, `account_id`, `start_date`, `renewal_date` (term end + 1 day), `seats`, `arr_usd`, `plan`, `synced_at`.\n\n### `renewal_outcomes` - one row per decided renewal\n`contract_id`, `account_id`, `renewal_date`, `outcome` (`renewed` / `churned`), `decided_at`, `synced_at`.\nRenewals not yet decided have no row.\n\n### `usage_weekly` - one row per account per week\n`account_id`, `week_start` (Monday), `active_users`, `api_calls`, `logins`, `synced_at`.\n\n### `support_tickets` - one row per ticket (current state)\n`ticket_id`, `account_id`, `opened_at`, `severity` (sev1-sev3), `category`, `closed_at` (null while open), `synced_at`.\n\n### `crm_opportunities` - one row per CRM opportunity (CRM v3 object)\n`opportunity_id`, `account_id`, `contract_id` (renewal opportunities only), `opportunity_type`\n(`renewal` / `expansion`), `lead_source` (`direct` / `partner`), `owner`, `created_at`, `stage`,\n`forecast_category` (Pipeline / Best Case / Commit / Omitted / Closed), `amount_usd`, `close_date`,\n`competitor`, `last_modified_at`, `synced_at`.\nField values are the record's current values as of the extract. `account_id`, `contract_id`,\n`opportunity_type`, `lead_source` and `created_at` never change after creation.\n\n### `crm_opportunity_field_history` - one row per change to a tracked opportunity field\n`history_id`, `opportunity_id`, `field` (`stage`, `forecast_category`, `amount_usd`, `close_date`,\n`competitor`), `old_value`, `new_value` (text), `changed_at` (CRM commit time), `synced_at`, `changed_by`.\nAppend-only audit trail. When an opportunity is created, one row is written per tracked field (empty\n`old_value`, `changed_at` = the opportunity's `created_at`); a later change from an empty value (e.g. a\nfirst competitor) also has an empty `old_value`. The audit trail has been replicated continuously since\n2019 (CRM v2 `crm_field_audit`, renamed at the CRM v3 cutover).\n\n### `cs_account_health` - one row per account (Customer Success object)\n`account_id`, `health_score` (0-100), `health_color` (Green >= 70, Yellow 50-69, Red < 50),\n`nps_last` (null if the account never answered), `csm_sentiment` (Positive / Neutral / Negative),\n`last_changed_at`, `synced_at`. Current values as of the extract.\nHealth scores are recalculated monthly; NPS surveys run quarterly; CSM sentiment is updated after\nbusiness reviews and escalations.\n\n### `cs_account_health_history` - one row per change to a tracked health field\n`history_id`, `account_id`, `field` (`health_score`, `health_color`, `nps_last`, `csm_sentiment`),\n`old_value`, `new_value` (text), `changed_at`, `synced_at`, `changed_by`. Append-only. The record's\ncreation writes one row per field (`changed_at` = account creation); later changes from an empty value\nalso have an empty `old_value`.\n\n### `warehouse_sync_log` - connector incidents and replays\n`source`, `event`, `started_at`, `ended_at`, `note`.\n\n## Decommissioned\n\n`crm_opportunity_daily_snapshot` and `cs_health_daily_snapshot` (one row per record per day, taken at\n00:00 UTC) were dropped on 2026-01-31 after the CRM v3 migration. See `docs/ops/2026-01_crm_v3_migration.md`.\n# INC-1874 - CRM change stream paused\n\nStatus: resolved. Severity 2. Data Platform on-call.\n\n- **2025-11-03 00:00 UTC**: the warehouse CRM connector stopped applying the change stream after a\n  schema change in CRM (new picklist value). Pipeline dashboards\n  showed no movement for two weeks.\n- **2025-11-18 03:10 UTC**: connector fixed and the two-week backlog replayed into the warehouse.\n- Follow-ups: schema-change alerting (DATA-871). Opportunity and Customer Success health data is now served from the\nv3 objects `crm_opportunities` and `cs_account_health`, which replace the v2 opportunity and health\ntables.\n\n- The daily snapshot jobs (`crm_opportunity_daily_snapshot`, `cs_health_daily_snapshot`) stop on\n  2026-01-31 and the tables are dropped (storage cost was ~40% of the CRM schema). Quarterly retraining runbook - renewal-risk\n\n1. Scheduler job `renewal_risk_quarterly_retrain` runs `python -m renewal_risk run --config config/pipeline.toml --as-of <quarter date>`.\n2. Owner reviews `reports/model_evaluation/eval_<as_of>.json` against the production model's report:\n   ROC AUC, PR AUC, calibration by decile, AUC by segment.\n3. Promotion needs: no metric regression beyond 0.02 ROC AUC, calibration within +/-5 points per decile\n   band, sign-off from the Customer Success analytics lead.\n4. Promote: copy the model artifact to the scoring service; daily scoring switches the next day.\n5. After go-live, `reports/monitoring/` tracks live performance as renewals are decided (about 90-120\n   days after the first scores).\n# Analytics warehouse - data dictionary (renewal-risk sources)\n\nThe warehouse is a replica fed by connectors. Every table has `synced_at`: the UTC time the row (or\nits latest version) was loaded into the warehouse. Source systems also record their own business\ntimestamps (`changed_at`, `opened_at`, `decided_at`, ...), which can be earlier than `synced_at`.\n\n## Load schedules\n\n| Source | Connector | Typical delay between source commit and `synced_at` |\n|--------|-----------|------------------------------------------------------|\n| Billing contracts | nightly batch | contracts exist before their start date |\n| Renewal outcomes | change stream | minutes |\n| Product usage | weekly rollup job | loaded Monday ~02:00 UTC for the week that closed on Sunday |\n| Support tickets | change stream | minutes |\n| CRM (CRM v3), direct-sales records | change stream | minutes; paused during replication incidents and replayed afterwards (see `warehouse_sync_log`, `docs/ops/`) |\n| CRM (CRM v3), partner-channel records (`lead_source = partner`) | partner integration, weekly batch | up to a week (Sunday 22:00 UTC batch) |\n| Customer Success platform | nightly batch | changes made on day D are loaded ~01:30 UTC on D+1 |\n\nRows loaded by one connector for one record are applied in the order they were committed in the source.\n\n## Tables\n\n### `accounts` - one row per account\n`account_id`, `segment` (Enterprise / Mid-Market / SMB), `region`, `industry`, `created_date`, `synced_at`.\nStatic firmographics.\n\n### `contracts` - one row per contract term\n`contract_id`, `account_id`, `start_date`, `renewal_date` (term end + 1 day), `seats`, `arr_usd`, `plan`, `synced_at`.\n\n### `renewal_outcomes` - one row per decided renewal\n`contract_id`, `account_id`, `renewal_date`, `outcome` (`renewed` / `churned`), `decided_at`, `synced_at`.\nRenewals not yet decided have no row.\n\n### `usage_weekly` - one row per account per week\n`account_id`, `week_start` (Monday), `active_users`, `api_calls`, `logins`, `synced_at`.\n\n### `support_tickets` - one row per ticket (current state)\n`ticket_id`, `account_id`, `opened_at`, `severity` (sev1-sev3), `category`, `closed_at` (null while open), `synced_at`.\n\n### `crm_opportunities` - one row per CRM opportunity (CRM v3 object)\n`opportunity_id`, `account_id`, `contract_id` (renewal opportunities only), `opportunity_type`\n(`renewal` / `expansion`), `lead_source` (`direct` / `partner`), `owner`, `created_at`, `stage`,\n`forecast_category` (Pipeline / Best Case / Commit / Omitted / Closed), `amount_usd`, `close_date`,\n`competitor`, `last_modified_at`, `synced_at`.\nField values are the record's current values as of the extract. `account_id`, `contract_id`,\n`opportunity_type`, `lead_source` and `created_at` never change after creation.\n\n### `crm_opportunity_field_history` - one row per change to a tracked opportunity field\n`history_id`, `opportunity_id`, `field` (`stage`, `forecast_category`, `amount_usd`, `close_date`,\n`competitor`), `old_value`, `new_value` (text), `changed_at` (CRM commit time), `synced_at`, `changed_by`.\nAppend-only audit trail. When an opportunity is created, one row is written per tracked field (empty\n`old_value`, `changed_at` = the opportunity's `created_at`); a later change from an empty value (e.g. a\nfirst competitor) also has an empty `old_value`. The audit trail has been replicated continuously since\n2019 (CRM v2 `crm_field_audit`, renamed at the CRM v3 cutover).\n\n### `cs_account_health` - one row per account (Customer Success object)\n`account_id`, `health_score` (0-100), `health_color` (Green >= 70, Yellow 50-69, Red < 50),\n`nps_last` (null if the account never answered), `csm_sentiment` (Positive / Neutral / Negative),\n`last_changed_at`, `synced_at`. Current values as of the extract.\nHealth scores are recalculated monthly; NPS surveys run quarterly; CSM sentiment is updated after\nbusiness reviews and escalations.\n\n### `cs_account_health_history` - one row per change to a tracked health field\n`history_id`, `account_id`, `field` (`health_score`, `health_color`, `nps_last`, `csm_sentiment`),\n`old_value`, `new_value` (text), `changed_at`, `synced_at`, `changed_by`. Append-only. The record's\ncreation writes one row per field (`changed_at` = account creation); later changes from an empty value\nalso have an empty `old_value`.\n\n### `warehouse_sync_log` - connector incidents and replays\n`source`, `event`, `started_at`, `ended_at`, `note`.\n\n## Decommissioned\n\n`crm_opportunity_daily_snapshot` and `cs_health_daily_snapshot` (one row per record per day, taken at\n00:00 UTC) were dropped on 2026-01-31 after the CRM v3 migration. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfabsSRoWM3ZbsTmK1D3v","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I found the problem and fixed it. v2.4 was trained and evaluated on CRM and Customer Success data from *after* each renewal had been decided, so the training labels leaked into the features. The 0.936 offline AUC was never real. Re-run on the current extract (as of 2026-08-15) with the fix, the model scores **ROC AUC 0.781, PR AUC 0.445, Brier 0.177**, which is in line with v2.3 (0.80 offline, 0.78 live) and v2.4's live 0.75.\n\n**What went wrong**\n- The CRM v3 migration (2.4.0) replaced the daily snapshot tables with readers on `crm_opportunities` and `cs_account_health`. Those tables only hold each record's current value, which for past renewals means the value after the outcome.\n- Renewal stage alone gave the answer away: in the v2.4 training data, every example at \"Closed Won\" renewed (899 of 899) and every one at \"Closed Lost\" churned (168 of 168). At 90 days before renewal, no opportunity is in either stage.\n- Every health and NPS value was the extract-day value too: `health_score` was wrong on 96% of examples and `nps_last` on 98%.\n- Production scoring was not affected, because it reads the live warehouse on the score date. So the model was trained on information it never sees when it scores, which is why the scores Sales and CS got were unusable. Its \"safe\" scores leaned on signals that are never present at scoring time.\n\n**The fix** (`src/renewal_risk/`)\n- **New `sources/point_in_time.py`:** rebuilds each CRM and health field as it stood at the prediction date, using the field history tables. A history row only counts if it was loaded into the warehouse before that date, not merely changed in the CRM by then. That covers the weekly partner batch and the INC-1874 backlog replayed on 2025-11-18.\n- **`features/pipeline_signals.py` and `features/health.py`:** now use those rebuilt values. An opportunity or health record not yet loaded at the prediction date gets the documented defaults.\n- **`sources/warehouse.py`:** loads the two history tables.\n- **`scoring.py`:** a renewal now counts as already decided only if its outcome was loaded before the score date, so scoring past dates gives the same answer production would have.\n- **Housekeeping:** version bumped to 2.4.2 with a `CHANGELOG.md` entry. Model spec, features, examples and split are unchanged, and nothing depends on a particular date, account or event.\n\n**How I checked it**\n- **Matches production:** I ran the original v2.4 model on the rebuilt features. It reproduced all 217 logged v2.4 production scores to within 5e-7, which is just the rounding in the log.\n- **Brute-force check:** a direct per-example SQL lookup on 738 examples, including every partner-channel and outage-window case, gave 0 mismatches.\n- **Fixed v2.4 run:** re-running the February training with the fix gives AUC 0.823, not 0.936. Its offline evaluation was overstated by about 0.11.\n- **Outputs:** same files, columns and grain as before (2,896 examples, one per renewal). Two runs gave byte-identical outputs, each in about 3 seconds. The report metrics match a recomputation from `eval_predictions.csv`.\n- **Other dates and scoring:** other `--as-of` dates (2024-11 to 2026-05) gave AUCs of 0.79–0.82. The `score` command works.\n\nThe default-date training outputs are regenerated (`artifacts/`, `eval_2026-08-15.json`, `latest.json`). My test runs overwrote the historical `eval_2026-02-15.json` and `eval_2025-08-15.json`, so I restored the original files; the warehouse and the monitoring reports were not touched.\n\n**Before you promote a Q3 model**\n- **Enterprise is weak:** ROC AUC for that segment is only 0.61, against about 0.81 for Mid-Market and SMB.\n- **Scores run high:** the balanced class weighting in the spec puts the mean score well above the churn rate (top decile averages 0.86 against 56% actual churn). That is why v2.4's live mean score was 0.34 against 17% churn. Under the runbook's ±5-point calibration rule this would need a calibration step or a decision on class weighting; I left the documented spec as it is."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1349,"cache_read_input_tokens":74687,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1349,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9924aeaa-b5a8-40ae-9928-5cfbafc22596","uuid":"2bde0366-ede5-46c8-85d1-d8c4c1d7e86b","timestamp":"2026-10-01T00:52:36.095Z","request_id":"req_011CfabsRdgen7toBo8abc7L"}
